  6968: function updateConnectionStatus(domain = "") {
  6969:   const connectionDot = document.getElementById("connectionDot");
  6970:   const connectionText = document.getElementById("connectionText");
  6971: 
  6972:   if (!connectionDot || !connectionText) return;
  6973: 
  6974:   if (domain) {
  6975:     connectionDot.classList.remove("disconnected");
  6976:     connectionDot.classList.add("connected");
  6977:     connectionText.textContent = "Connected";
  6978:   } else {
  6979:     connectionDot.classList.remove("connected");
  6980:     connectionDot.classList.add("disconnected");
  6981:     connectionText.textContent = "Disconnected";
  6982:   }
  6983: }

  6871:         const workspaceId = getCurrentWorkspaceIdSafe();
  6872:

  6874:           alert("No active workspace found.");

  6887:           body: JSON.stringify({ workspace_id: workspaceId })
  6888:         })
  6889:           .then(res => res.json().then(data => ({ ok: res.ok, data })))
  6890:           .then(({ ok, data }) => {
  6891:             if (!ok || !data.ok) {
  6892:               throw new Error(data.detail || "Could not disconnect domain");
  6893:             }
  6894: 
  6895:             localStorage.removeItem("lc_domain");
  6896:             window.LC_CONNECTED_DOMAIN = "";
  6897:             window.CURRENT_DOMAIN = "";
  6898: 
  6899:             updateConnectionStatus("");

  3614:         document.querySelectorAll(".lc-cw-option").forEach(showBox);
  3615:         hideBox(btn);
  3616:       });
  3617:     }
  3618:     return btn;
  3619:   }

  3621:   function setWorkspaceStartMode(mode){
  3622:     mode = String(mode || "domain").trim();
  3623: 
  3624:     const domainFields = document.getElementById("domainStartFields");
  3625:     const sitemapFields = document.getElementById("sitemapStartFields");
  3626:     const domainInput = document.getElementById("domainInput");
  3627:     const sitemapInput = document.getElementById("sitemapInput");
  3628: 
  3629:     if (mode === "domain") {
  3630:       showBox(domainFields);
  3631:       hideBox(sitemapFields);
  3632:       if (domainInput) domainInput.disabled = false;
  3633:       if (sitemapInput) sitemapInput.disabled = true;
  3634:     }
  3635: 
  3636:     if (mode === "sitemap") {
  3637:       hideBox(domainFields);
  3638:       showBox(sitemapFields);
  3639:       if (domainInput) domainInput.disabled = true;
  3640:       if (sitemapInput) sitemapInput.disabled = false;
  3641:     }
  3642: 
  3643:     if (mode === "blank") {
  3644:       hideBox(domainFields);
  3645:       hideBox(sitemapFields);
  3646:       if (domainInput) domainInput.disabled = true;
  3647:       if (sitemapInput) sitemapInput.disabled = true;
  3648:     }
  3649: 
  3650:     window.workspaceMode = mode;
  3651:     window.LC_WORKSPACE_START_MODE = mode;
  3652: 
  3653:     document.querySelectorAll('input[name="workspaceStartMode"]').forEach(function(input){
  3654:       const card = input.closest(".lc-cw-option");
  3655:       if (!card) return;
  3656: 
  3657:       const active = input.value === mode;
  3658:       card.classList.toggle("active", active);
  3659: 
  3660:       if (lockedMode) {
  3661:         active ? showBox(card) : hideBox(card);
  3662:       }
  3663:     });
  3664: 
  3665:     const changeBtn = ensureChangeButton();
  3666:     if (changeBtn) {
  3667:       lockedMode ? showBox(changeBtn) : hideBox(changeBtn);
  3668:     }
  3669:   }
  3670: 
  3671:   function bindWorkspaceStartMode(){
  3672:     ensureChangeButton();
  3673: 
  3674:     document.querySelectorAll('input[name="workspaceStartMode"]').forEach(function(input){
  3675:       const card = input.closest(".lc-cw-option");
  3676: 
  3677:       function choose(){
  3678:         input.checked = true;
  3679:         lockedMode = true;
  3680:         setWorkspaceStartMode(input.value);
  3681:       }
  3682: 
  3683:       input.addEventListener("change", choose);
  3684:       if (card) card.addEventListener("click", choose);
  3685:     });
  3686: 
  3687:     const checked = document.querySelector('input[name="workspaceStartMode"]:checked');
  3688:     setWorkspaceStartMode(checked ? checked.value : "domain");
  3689:   }
  3690: 
  3691:   if (document.readyState === "loading") {
  3692:     document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", bindWorkspaceStartMode);
  3693:   } else {
  3694:     bindWorkspaceStartMode();
  3695:   }
  3696: 
  3697:   window.LC_setWorkspaceStartMode = setWorkspaceStartMode;
  3698: })();
  3699: </script>
